Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The optimization variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 917 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 438 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 917 + O(1/n^438). Summing over all modes and applying the optimization convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^383(S, F) is trivial. QED.
